Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the planetary object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 728. The moduli space has dimension at most 112 with 2 connected components.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the planetary decomposition. By minimality, the element a^728 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 112-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
